| The history never carries it | `conversationHistory` copies `role` and `content` and nothing else, and a test locks it with an entry that holds a report, a choice and timings (`history.test.ts`). The targeting line is part of *Copier la conversation* (what she copies is what she saw) and of nothing the model reads |
| Settings | The group « Connaissances » of the dialog: **Ciblage intelligent** (`Désactivé` / `Suggestions seulement` / `Automatique`, the same `knowledge_mode`), the health modules, **Masquer les noms dans les suggestions**, and an **Avancé** disclosure with the five thresholds of master section 8, the timing log and **Afficher le panneau de diagnostics**. `knowledge_hide_names_in_suggestions` (default true) is added to `settings.rs` now and read by the suggestions of lot 9. The two shares are typed as percentages and stored as fractions; every number is committed when the field loses focus and pulled back into the bounds Rust clamps to, which a test reads from `settings.rs` so the two cannot drift |
| The developer panel | `DiagnosticsPanel`, only when `showDiagnostics` is on, under the **last** answer: this answer's timings and counts (`RetrievalDiagnostics`) and the recorded questions added up (`knowledge_diagnostics(500)`, one cheap query when the panel appears and after each answer, nothing on a timer). Numbers, units and catalogue words only; a test checks that no value can be anything else |
| What Rust gained | `ReportCandidate` carries `documents` and `workbooks` (the files of the selection that name it, one query for all the offered names, `QueryEntityDiagnostic` unchanged), `ReportAmbiguity` and `ReportPossible` carry the `span` of the question, `Settings.knowledge_hide_names_in_suggestions`, the module `knowledge::picker` and its command. The lot file listed no Rust: the picker it describes needs a command that did not exist. The span is the user's own words, shown on screen only and in no log |
| Left for later | ~~**Voir les connaissances**~~ (drawn by lot 8, under the analysis summary of both cards). The analysis summary lines of lots 4 and 5 were already drawn by both cards. Question suggestions and the use of `knowledge_hide_names_in_suggestions` (lot 9). macOS has not been run |

## Settled by KB lot 8, the knowledge management dialog (12 October 2026)

| Subject | Decision |
| --- | --- |
| What it is | `KnowledgeDialog`, an administrative place to **see, correct and extend** what the knowledge base holds. Opened from the settings (« Connaissances » → **Gérer…**) and from the link **Voir les connaissances** under the analysis summary of either folder card; **never reachable from the conversation**. Four tabs: **Entités** (list, search, filters, pages of 50, the sheet of the chosen entity), **À vérifier** (the pairs that might be one entity, and the library-health counts), **Types et rôles** (read-only), **Réinitialiser**. Every sentence is a catalogue key (`manage.*`); Rust sends ids, counts and machine codes |
| Provenance | The sheet of an entity answers « why does the product think this belongs to this source »: its names (with kind and origin), the mentions **grouped by source** (document or spreadsheet, the file), each with its place (« page 3, passage 2 », « feuille « Mars », colonne « Medecin », lignes 2, 5, 9 »), a short passage read from the chunk the index already stores, the method (« titre suivi d'un nom complet »), the confidence, the name that matched, the role; then the links read between two columns, the details, the pairs, and **how the columns of each spreadsheet were read or why they were not**. At most 200 mentions are listed; the sheet says how many there are in all |
| One transaction, ids in the log | Each write opens `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`, does everything, appends one line to `kb_ops_log` and commits; a busy database gives `knowledge_unavailable` after the 3-second wait and changes nothing (the dialog offers a retry). The log holds a kind and ids, never a name or a passage |
| Nothing heavy, nothing in the background | A write that adds or removes a name raises `kb_meta.gazetteer_epoch`, which makes the documents **due**; they are read again, from the stored text and with no request to the AI, **inside the next Analyse the user starts** (`backfill::refresh_documents`, already run at the end of every Analyse). The lot file asked for a `knowledge_refresh` with progress; there is none, by the owner's rule of 11 October 2026 (`AGENTS.md`, "No costly silent background work"). Every write returns `documentsDue` and the dialog says « N documents seront relus à la prochaine analyse » |
| Create, edit, names | A hand-made entity is `active` and `manual`; the types offered are person, organization, location, item, term (**never identifier**: found by its shape, and a personal one is stored as a keyed hash). Editing an **automatic** entity turns it manual (it survives re-analysis and garbage collection) and keeps its old name as an alias, so documents that still write the old name lead to it. A name must be free for its type (`knowledge_name_taken`), not empty or only a title (`knowledge_name_invalid`). A new name is reported when it is already recognised for another entity (`sharedWith`): a question that uses it will then ask which one. Only a **manual** alias can be removed; an automatic one is rewritten by the next Analyse. A detail key must be a machine id and not a personal identifier kind (`knowledge_operation_refused`). A name under three letters is found in documents but never read in a question (`QueryEntityDetector::may_be_a_name`): the dialog says so while it is typed |
| Merge | A redirect, as lot 1 made it. **Entities of two types are refused**, not confirmed (`knowledge_merge_refused`), contrary to the lot file's « confirm when the types differ »: the owner's rule of 11 October 2026 says a module never excludes or transforms an entity type, and a merge across types would. The merge dialog offers only the same type; the default survivor is the entity seen more often, then the older (`defaultSurvivor`); she can swap them before confirming. Accepting a possible match is a merge, dismissing it is final (a later pass does not write the pair again) and can be undone |
| Split | For two people written with the same name. Chosen mentions move to a new **manual** entity whose name **must differ** from every name held (two entities with one name are one ambiguity for the scanner, which would then place neither and let the next Analyse drop the original's mentions). Each moved mention becomes manual and carries `kb_mentions.split_from` (an additive column, added by `ensure_schema` to a base from lots 1 to 7), and `apply_delta` skips an automatic mention at a place where one was moved away: **a re-reading does not undo a split**. Refused when it would move none or all of an entity's mentions. The new entity gets only its own name as an alias (a surname or an initial would collide with the original) |
| Delete, restore | Delete is the tombstone of lot 1 (mentions and relations go, the name stays so the next Analyse does not recreate it). **Restore turns the entity manual**: an automatic entity with no mention would be collected as an orphan before the next Analyse could find its documents again. Its mentions return with that Analyse, for documents; a workbook's mentions return only when the workbook is analysed again (workbooks are not made due by a name list) |
| Undo | `undo_last_operation` reverses the newest operation that is not itself an undo and not already undone, for: create (only while the entity holds nothing), add a name, merge, unmerge, delete, restore, split, dismiss a pair. An undo writes an `undo` line listing the lines it caused, so the next undo walks back one more operation and never toggles. **Removing a name, editing and setting a detail cannot be undone**: undoing them would need the text she typed, and the log holds ids only; they are confirmed (or are one click to redo). An operation after which the base moved on (an entity found in a document since) is refused with `knowledge_operation_refused` and changes nothing. A reset is a barrier |
| Reset | `knowledge_reset(includeManual)`. Without it: the sources and what only they held up go; manual entities, names, details **and tombstones** stay (a deleted false positive must not come back). With it: everything, including the log. Either way the documents are due again and the next Analyse rebuilds the base from the stored text. An automatic entity that holds a manual name (« Jean Dupont » with the name « Jeannot ») stays, with no mention, until that Analyse. Files, chunks, inventories and cell caches are never touched; the dialog's text explains the difference with the two folder Resets |
| Rust | New `knowledge/manage.rs` (reads and writes) and 21 commands (`knowledge_overview`, `_list_entities`, `_entity_sheet`, `_possible_matches`, `_health`, `_types_and_roles`, `_create_entity`, `_edit_entity`, `_add_alias`, `_remove_alias`, `_set_attribute`, `_remove_attribute`, `_merge`, `_unmerge`, `_split`, `_delete_entity`, `_restore_entity`, `_accept_match`, `_dismiss_match`, `_undo`, `_reset`), all `#[tauri::command(async)]` so a busy database never freezes the window. Four machine codes: `knowledge_name_invalid`, `knowledge_name_taken`, `knowledge_operation_refused`, `knowledge_nothing_to_undo` |
| Known limits shown by the dialog | Defect KBD-13 is now visible: the method of most mentions reads « nom déjà connu de la base » because the end-of-pass re-reading rewrites the method (deferred). Candidates (KBD-11) are listed, tagged **À confirmer**, and can be deleted or edited. Hiding an **automatic** alias (the lot file's « can be hidden ») is not done: every reader of aliases (resolver, gazetteer, prefix search) would have to honour a flag; deleting or splitting the entity is the remedy for now |
| **Owner answers, 12 October 2026 (binding, to be found again if they cause trouble)** | (1) **A merge across two entity types stays refused** (`knowledge_merge_refused`); the dialog offers only the same type. If it causes trouble later: change the entity's type with *Modifier* first, then merge; do not lift the refusal in `store::merge_entities` without the owner. (2) **A restored entity becomes `manual`** (`manage::restore_and_keep`): it then survives the reset that keeps what the user typed, and is not collected as an orphan. If it causes trouble later: the alternative was to keep it automatic and let it vanish when no document names it. (3) **A name under three letters is accepted** and found in documents, but never read in a question (`QueryEntityDetector::may_be_a_name`, lot 6); the dialog warns while it is typed. (4) **Hiding an automatic alias is not built**, on purpose: the results are good and the product must not grow more complex; a wrong automatic alias is dealt with by deleting or splitting its entity. If it matters later: a `hidden` flag on `kb_aliases` that the resolver, the gazetteer, `prefix_search` and the query detector would all have to honour. (5) **KBD-13 (the method of a mention is rewritten to « nom déjà connu de la base ») is not fixed before lot 11**: it only blurs the wording of the provenance, it changes no answer, no narrowing and no promotion (both thresholds sit below 0.85); it stays in the post-KB fix battery (`defects-register.md`) |

## Settled by KB lot 9, services on the shared vocabulary (12 October 2026)

| Subject | Decision |
| --- | --- |
| Look of the **i** | It always has the look of a hovered button (the page background, the text colour, a border; the chip's is bordered in the primary green), taken from the theme tokens so that the dark theme needs nothing of its own. Its first look (transparent) vanished on the highlighted row and on the chip. The sheet's style rules have their own class names (`entity-card`): the first version used `card`, which the folder cards use, and squashed the two cards of « Réglages » |
| Where the suggestions are drawn | In the empty conversation and **at the bottom of the card** « Dossier des documents » (or, when no document is selected, of « Dossier des données »), **outside the analysis summary**: the summary is empty after a pass that changed nothing and sits inside a disclosure, and the first version, drawn there, vanished after a second « Analyser » |
| What it is | Five **read-only** services over the names of the base, none of which stores or learns anything (invariant I3): `knowledge::surface` (ranked names for speech and autocomplete), `knowledge::repair` (proposals to repair a transcript), `knowledge::suggest` (questions worth asking), `knowledge::sheet` (what the selection says about one name) and `knowledge::guard` (names found in a text about to leave). Two have a face in the window (the suggestions and the sheet); `surface`, `repair` and `guard` are Rust APIs with no command, for the speech work and the connectors that come later. The only edits outside the new files are additive: `commands.rs` (two commands, one cache in `AppState`), `tabular_answer.rs` (`WorkbookChoice`, `selected_workbook_choices`, `open_single_sheet_workbook`), `query.rs` (`words_of` and `Word` made `pub(crate)`) |
| Surface forms | `surface_forms(selection, purpose, limit, prefix)`. **Selection first:** with a selection only the names mentioned in its files come back (I7); `None` means the whole base and no command passes it. Weight = 10 per file of the selection + 0.2 per mention (at most 50 counted) + 2 x the best confidence, + 5 for a name typed by hand, divided by 10 for an unconfirmed one; the order is **confirmed names, then weight, then name**, so it is the same every time. **Speech hints** leave unconfirmed names out and stop at 40 names **and** at 200 estimated tokens (`estimated_tokens`: the characters of the displays plus 2 each, divided by 3, rounded up, said to be an estimate). The spoken form is a typed `spoken_form` attribute, else the display with each title spelled out by the pack (`Dr` as `docteur`), else the display. **No recency is stored.** The composer's `@` list stays `picker::search_entities` (it also reports the names outside the selection); the lot file's « thin command over `surface` » was not done, because the picker is accepted and tested and the lot 8 hand-over says it stays |
| Transcript repair | `repair::propose(transcript, selection, case_reliable)`. It looks at a word that is capitalised (if the engine's case can be trusted) or follows a title, has at least four letters and is neither a stop-word nor a title; a run of up to three words is read as one name when its first word qualifies (`Du pont`). A run that is **already a known name is left alone**. Candidates are the **confirmed names of the selection** only. A candidate is `phonetic` (same sound key, within 1 edit up to 6 letters and 2 above), `alias` (the same letters as a name written with other spacing) or `edit` (one edit on 6 letters or more, same first letter, other sound). One strong candidate (`phonetic` or `alias`) is a **suggest**; several candidates, or an `edit`, are an **ask**; no candidate gives **no proposal** (the lot file's `None` action is the absence of a proposal). When several runs start at one word, the strongest wins and, on a tie, the shortest (`Duran a` is `Duran`). It never rewrites the text and never writes to the base. Contract file: `tests/fixtures/knowledge/repair-fr.json` (six must-propose cases, ten must-not-change, two without case), reused by the speech work |
| Question suggestions | `suggest::suggest` returns **machine data** (`templateId` and slots), the sentence is `suggestions.<id>` in the catalogues (and `<id>InFile` when the selection holds several workbooks, so that the product does not ask which one). Eight kinds: `entity_documents`, `entity_rows_count`, `entity_total`, `entity_period_count`, `entity_compare_docs_data`, `top_group`, `each_document`, `folder_count`. **Shown only if answerable:** documents need the name in at least two documents of the selection; a table kind is run as the equivalent `Operation` (with a `FilterSpec` on the cell the name was read from) through `tabular::engine::execute` on the cached typed workbook, and must give a value; at most **5 dry runs** per refresh (`MAX_DRY_RUNS`); the month and year of the period kind are read from the data, from a real `Date` column only. The sentences are proved by `tests/knowledge_surfaces.rs`, which reads both catalogues, fills every sentence and puts it through the real folder router and the real tabular classifier |
| What a suggestion may not be | **Not a document question when two workbooks and documents are selected** (defect KBD-21: the product would answer « Quel classeur ? »). **`folder_count` only with no workbook selected** (KBD-23: a table would answer with its row count). **Not for a name that reads as a file name** (KBD-18). **Not for a table with several reachable sheets** (a sentence names no sheet). **Not a total of a year column or of an unsettled date column** (`example_columns` already skips years; `top_group` also skips `ambiguous_date`, KBD-22). Each of these is a test. **The sentence says only the words that point at the one cell** (`askable_words`): a column written `Dr Jean Dupont`, `Dr Paul Martin` is asked as `Jean Dupont`, because the tables read a word shared by several values of a column as an equality on the word itself and answer 0 rows (KBD-24, found by walking the human test's own fixtures) |
| Names hidden by default | `knowledge_hide_names_in_suggestions` (true) keeps every kind that names somebody out of the result; the result says `namesHidden` when the selection holds names, and the interface then offers **Afficher les suggestions avec des noms**. The reveal is the user's own click, lasts the session (a `useState` in `App.tsx`, never stored, the setting is not changed) and can be undone with **Masquer à nouveau les noms**. Mode `off`: no suggestion at all |
| When it looks | A suggestion is looked up when a screen that draws it is shown (the empty conversation, the bottom of a folder card), 500 ms after the selection settles, never at launch and never while an Analyse is writing; the command is `async`; a failed lookup is no suggestion. It reads the cached workbooks (as a question does) and **never starts the reading of a large file**: a workbook whose cells are not cached (an Analyse empties that cache) is read for a suggestion only when it is at most 2 MB (`COLD_READ_LIMIT_BYTES`); a larger one gives no table suggestion until a question about it has filled the cache (test `a_suggestion_does_not_start_the_reading_of_a_large_workbook`). A refresh is answered from memory the second time (`SuggestionCache`: eight screens, keyed by the selection, the language, the visibility of names and a fingerprint of the base). **Owner rule of 11 October 2026 (no costly silent background work):** this is a read started by looking at a screen, of the cost of a question's routing; it reads no document and runs nothing on the AI. Measured in the lot report |
| What a click does | Sends the sentence as a question, as if it had been typed (the empty conversation: at once; at the bottom of a card: `App` hands it to the chat panel, which sends it when nothing is being answered). Buttons are disabled while an answer is written |
| The sheet of a name | `sheet::entity_sheet` and the command **`knowledge_entity_card`** (not `knowledge_entity_sheet`: lot 8 already owns that name for the management dialog's sheet, and the dialog's component is `EntitySheet.tsx`; the lot 9 component is `EntityCardDialog.tsx`). Aliases (the canonical name left out), roles, the files **of the selection** (documents with mention counts and pages, tables with sheet, column and rows), **a count only** for the files outside it (never which), the links stated by the files of the selection, and the details that are not personal identifiers (keys `identifier:*` and the ids and kinds of the packs' identifier schemes are left out). Opened from a chip or from the `@` list by the small **i** button (the management dialog keeps its own sheet). `sheet::files_for_entities` is the same query as a Rust API for the calendar and the organiser; no command, because nothing needs one yet |
| The outbound guard | `guard::OutboundGuard::build` reads **every** live name and alias of the base, candidates included, **globally and on purpose**: a guard limited to the selection would let through the name of a person whose file is not ticked. It is the one exception to selection scoping and is recorded in `PRIVACY-AND-SECURITY.md`. A hit is a span of the text, an entity id and a strength: **strong** for a full name (two words or more, not an initial, not a surname alone) or an identifier, **weak** otherwise; a full name wins over the surname inside it; a name shared by two people is one hit per person. Case, accents and the reordered form do not matter; a personal identifier is found through the keyed hash. `strip` replaces the spans once. No command returns any of it to the interface. Build a guard per request, not per launch |
| Not built | The optional « also in these documents » row after an answer; the sheet from the management dialog; the thin `surface` command for the picker; speech capture and engines. A suggestion for a table with several reachable sheets |
| **Owner answers, 12 October 2026 (binding, to be found again when solutions are sought)** | (1) **Nothing is affirmed that cannot be proved.** The block is titled **« Suggestions »** and says **« Questions suggérées à partir de vos fichiers »** (it said « elle a une réponse », dropped). Stay prudent in every sentence the interface says about what the product knows or can answer; KBD-21 to KBD-24 are the reason, and solutions are to be looked for later. (2) **The workbook's name stays in the sentence** when several workbooks are selected (« ... dans rdv.csv »); same prudence, **to be revisited later if it blocks** (KBD-04: a name that holds a year or a month, such as `ventes-2025.xlsx`, puts that word in the question). (3) **The small « i » stays in several places** (chip and `@` list); if it proves too intrusive it will be removed later. (4) **A workbook above 2 MB that is not cached gives no suggestion until a question has read it: accepted for now** (2 MB is already a large spreadsheet; to be watched in use, revisit if it blocks). |

## KB lot 10, activity profiles: built, tried by the owner, withdrawn (12 October 2026)

**Owner decision, 12 October 2026: the lot is withdrawn entirely. Nothing of it is in the product.** The "module" notion (what a vocabulary pack is for, how it is chosen and switched off, which packs exist) comes back later, **with precise use cases**, not before.

| Subject | Decision |
| --- | --- |
| What was built and tried | A card « Profil détecté » in « Réglages » that counted, while a source was read, the words pointing at each shipped pack, and suggested the packs the library resembled (« Santé : France 36 %, Droit : France 32 % ») with **Activer ce vocabulaire** and **Ignorer**. Code, tests and a human protocol were written and the owner ran the protocol: the system worked as designed (report: `lots/lot-10-activity-profiles.md`) |
| Why it is withdrawn | (1) **A vocabulary accepted from the card could not be switched off.** Only the two health packs have a tick in « Réglages »; legal and accounting had none, so the card created a state the user could not leave. The health ticks are named « Module santé » while the card said « Santé : France », so nobody saw they were the same thing. (2) **The benefit is small.** A pack changes four things, all at analysis time and none in an answer: the identifiers it recognises (health: the social security number; legal: a case number; accounting: SIRET and VAT number), the meaning of a handful of column headers, a few words that are never names, and the role labels of the knowledge dialog. Titles and organisation markers are read whatever is active. For the pilot the health pack is already on by default, and legal and accounting would change next to nothing she could see. (3) **Nothing depends on it.** No other feature reads a profile; the uses the lot file listed (more table suggestions for a data-oriented library, recommending connectors) were never built and are not needed now. (4) It had a cost: counting at every analysis, `CURRENT_KB_VERSION` raised to 2 (every analysed source read again), detection terms removed from the packs, and a modelling limit (country packs of one domain cannot share their general words, see « To remember ») |
| What stays as it was | `kb_source_signals` and `kb_profile` stay in the schema, **empty and unused** (lot 1 created them for this lot; removing a table is a migration for nothing). `detection_terms` stays an accepted, ignored field of the pack format, and the shipped packs keep their original terms. `SignalDraft` and `KnowledgeDelta.signals` stay, always empty. `CURRENT_KB_VERSION` stays 1. The only tick in « Réglages » is « Santé : France » / « Santé : Suisse » (`knowledgePacks`), as in lot 9; legal and accounting packs exist but are reachable only by editing `settings.json` |
| To remember if the notion returns | Start from use cases, not from the mechanism. Then: **every pack the user can turn on must be listed, with the same name, where it can be turned off**; the country packs of one domain share their general words (a Swiss library written with general medical words looks like `health-fr`: a `<domain>` pack that the country packs refine is the way, `health-eu` being the natural place); a detection term must not be a base stop-word and two packs of a domain must not share a term; turning a pack on must start no work (the next Analyse the user starts reads again), by the owner's rule of 11 October 2026; first thresholds measured were 20 files and 25 %. The work is kept **locally and outside git** in `data/withdrawn-lot-10/` (a patch of the tracked files, the new files, the previous status file) |
| Effect on the programme | Lot 11 no longer has a lot 10 to check. It validates lots 0 to 9. The tag `kb-after-lot-10` is not created. The milestone K-D (« lots 8 to 10 ») is reached by lots 8 and 9 |

## Out of scope until the pilot holds

Fine-tuning, mobile applications, a multi-practice hosted service, autonomous overnight operation, a cloud
fallback model on real files, a server-side case index, irreversible actions without review, a second shared
profession, wide-area multi-site access outside a practice VPN.

Asking a second profession questions is not opening one. Discovery findings wait in `docs/DISCOVERY.md` until
the GP pilot holds.

## Settled by KB lot 11, validation (12 October 2026)

Lot 11 adds no screen, no setting, no command and no line of product code. What it decided concerns how the knowledge base is judged and what is done with the result; the figures are in `docs/test-reports/knowledge-base-pass-1/04-final-report.md` and `03-release-gate.md`.

| Subject | Decision |
| --- | --- |
| What the lot is | A **library with a ground truth** (`fixtures/knowledge-sandbox`, generated by `scripts/gen-knowledge-sandbox.py`, deterministic, versioned at 200 documents, 1 000 generated on demand into `data/`), the tools that measure the base against it (`tests/knowledge_validation*.rs`, `tests/common/validation.rs`), a script for the owner (`human-tests/lot-11-validation.md` with `lot11_helper.py`), the final documentation (`docs/KNOWLEDGE-BASE.md`), and a register of what was found, **fixed in none of it** |
| The library | Invented people of France and French-speaking Switzerland and of abroad, **confusables** (the Dupont and Martin families, a cardiologist with a patient's name, sound-alike surnames, given name and surname swapped, particles, apostrophes, `Dre`), organisations with and without a marker word, acronyms, identifiers (social security and AVS numbers with a valid key, IBAN FR and CH, invoice, contract, policy, telephone, e-mail), scans at three degrees of badness, 100 to 290-page documents with planted sentences, **formats the product does not read** (ODT, ODP, RTF, EML, VTT, PPTX, XLS, PPT stand-in, ODS), spreadsheets saved among the documents, workbooks with split `Nom`/`Prénom`, CP1252, free-note sheets and a `Nom` column of codes. Tiers are prefixes of one stream (30, 100, 200, 500, 1 000): a result at 30 is comparable with the same question at 200 |
| What is measured, and how | Names found, merged and missed (by type, by shape, by format); the files kept and dropped for every question, in the three modes, replaying what `sourced_answer` does before the model (router, selection, knowledge step); **recall of the files that hold the answer**; leaks (asserted zero); time by stage on fake vectors from 10 to 1 000 files and on the real stack (embedding model, bundled Tesseract and pdfium, a few real answers); what the real OCR reads by degree of scan |
| Thresholds are not assertions | Until the owner has seen the numbers and agreed them (lot file): the tests assert the invariants only (a file outside the selection is never searched; `off` and `suggest` never narrow; an ambiguous or unknown name never narrows; a named file and a selection of fewer than 8 files are left alone) |
| The release gate is read on the files that hold the answer | The base-off search always cites six passages, so on a small library most of what it cites for a named person is filler, and « of the files the answer cites with the base off, how many does the reduced set keep » is low by construction (31.3 % on 30 documents for a reduction that keeps 91.6 % of the files that hold the answer, on the real stack). **Proposed amendment, for the owner to accept** (defect KBD-34): the gate is read on the cited files that hold the answer (the sandbox's ground truth; on her library, the protocol's « un fichier pertinent raté »), the raw figure is reported beside it |
| `auto` stays optional | **Not made the default.** The reasons are in `03-release-gate.md`. The owner's own inclination (stay on « Suggestions seulement ») is what the evidence of this lot supports; the decision is hers, after she has played the script |
| Defects | Every defect found is a row of the register (KBD-25 to KBD-38 added by this lot) and a proposal of the **post-KB fix battery** (final report, section 9): none fixed here |
| CI | `.github/workflows/ci.yml` runs `cargo test --lib` only, so no integration test of the programme runs in CI. **Proposed, owner to decide**: add `cargo test --tests` (every integration test that is not `#[ignore]`; about eleven minutes on the development PC) as a second step on both runners, once a first run on the macOS runner has been read. Not changed here |
| The lot's own rules | The generator writes an `ocr_level` and a ground truth for every scan; **a fixture is a fictional file** and `fixtures/knowledge-sandbox/real/` does not exist; `data/knowledge-sandbox-1000` is never versioned. The script changes the mode through « Réglages » and never edits a file by hand: the helper writes the folders and the settings, and every command is written in full |

## Settled after KB lot 11, the owner's decisions on the evidence (12 October 2026)

Read after `docs/test-reports/knowledge-base-pass-1/03-release-gate.md`. The owner has run no human test and no git command yet; these are decisions on the measured figures.

| Subject | Decision |
| --- | --- |
| The verdict | **Accepted.** The release gate is not met; `Automatique` stays optional and `Suggestions seulement` stays the default. The measures of lot 11 are the reason |
| Gate item 2, rewritten (KBD-34) | « At least 100 real questions recorded in `suggest` on the owner's library; of the **cited files that hold the answer**, at least 98 % are in the candidate set; every miss is classified (`not_linked_to_the_name`, `capped`, `no_mention_in_file`, `weak_form_only`, `carry_over`, `entity_not_resolved`) and fixed or accepted in writing; the raw figure (every cited file) is reported beside it and is not gated. » Also required: **zero** wrong confident entity on the sandbox, zero file outside the selection |
| The fixes come before the big human test | The post-KB fix battery (final report, section 9) is applied **now**, in batches on new branches cut from the lot 11 commit and merged into `kb/integration`, so that the KB is operational in production when the owner judges it. Launcher: `docs/SESSION-KB-FIX-04-battery.md` |
| Switzerland first | French-speaking Switzerland is the privileged target for presenting the product and starting to sell it. Every fix that concerns it comes first (`Dre`, the AVS number read as a file name, `CHF 1'450.00`, hospitals and insurers without a marker word, names with an apostrophe, the formats a practice receives) and the whole KB must be optimised for it |
| CI | **Yes**: `cargo test --tests` joins `cargo test --lib` on both runners, Windows and macOS. The macOS side cannot be played yet; the Mac mini M5 Pro arrives in a few days, and everything written meanwhile must run there (no platform `cfg`, no Windows path literal in a test, no Windows-only tool in a helper). A macOS part is added to the final protocol |
| `kb/integration` is **never merged into `main`** | `main` stays the product without the KB so that the value of the KB can be compared with the product without it. `main` receives the KB only if the owner decides it later, after the comparison. This replaces « `main` receives the programme » in the milestone K-E |
| Every branch and tag is kept | Local and remote, so that the product can be tested at every stage of the integration and unnecessary or counter-productive complexity can be spotted. Report: `docs/test-reports/knowledge-base-pass-1/05-branches-and-stages.md` |
| The big human test | Rewritten after the fixes, with the Swiss part, the macOS part and a results book (one block per step), as a file the owner applies to the letter and reports from: nothing is asked in chat |

## Settled by KB fix 4, batch 1, Switzerland first (12 October 2026)

Branch `fix/kb-swiss-first`, cut from `kb-after-lot-11`; fixes F-00, F-01, F-02, F-03, F-11, F-14, F-16, F-24 and the summary line of F-20 of the battery (`docs/test-reports/knowledge-base-pass-1/04-final-report.md`, section 9). Report and figures: `docs/test-reports/knowledge-base-pass-1/lots/fix-04-batch-1-swiss-first.md`. Each fix was proved by a test seen failing first.

| Subject | Decision |
| --- | --- |
| CI runs the integration targets (F-00) | `.github/workflows/ci.yml` runs `cargo test --tests` after `cargo test --lib`, on Windows and macOS. A target that needs something a runner lacks is fixed, never skipped |
| Swiss titles (F-02, KBD-26) | `Dre`, `Dresse`, `Docteure`, `Professeure` are strong titles of the French base; `Pre` is a **weak** title (it is also the word `pre` once the accent is folded: it needs a capital and a capitalised word after it). Data only. `CURRENT_KB_VERSION` is 2, so documents are read again from their stored text at the next Analyse the user starts, never in the background |
| A title and a surname outrank the surname alone, in a question (F-02) | `la Dre Vuagniaux` is the person the base has seen written `Dre Vuagniaux`, not the patient who shares the surname. Only when the title-and-surname form is stored for exactly that title; at ingestion nothing changes. Two doctors who share the surname and the title stay a question (« lequel ? ») |
| A place never narrows the documents (F-03, KBD-29) | A `location` entity does not drive the reduction of the documents side (a chip the user chose still does); it still reduces the tables, where the column is the evidence. The cap of 15 is unchanged and still applies to people and organisations (batch 3) |
| A file name is a short word with a letter (F-16, KBD-35) | A token with a dot is reported as a missing file only when its extension is 1 to 5 letters or digits with at least one letter. `756.3052.5778.43` (AVS), `6.126.452-9` (policy), `04.50.12.34.56` and `jean.dupont@email.com` are identifiers: the question goes to the documents |
| An acronym is what the question is about (F-16) | A word of 2 to 6 capitals in a sentence that is not all capitals is a name typed on purpose: the folder router leaves the question to the documents (`Quels documents concernent HUG ?`). The index cannot answer « is this a word of the corpus » for words under four letters, which is why the corpus test alone could not |
| Swiss amounts (F-24, KBD-38) | `CHF`, `Fr.`, `SFr.` (any case, before or after) are currency symbols; `'` and `’` group thousands like a space and are never a decimal mark; the sign may follow the currency. The engine stays the authority on a number. `1'450.-` forms are not read (KBD-40) |
| A column named with two words is a name first (F-11, KBD-01) | The question is read without the column's words (one run) before the « rows » vocabulary is looked for, and the operation words that precede the name are kept. A one-word column keeps today's reading |
| A workbook named in a question is a reference (F-14, KBD-04) | The path or file name with its extension, or the name as one hyphenated token, is taken out of the text the table reader sees, typed or from the « which workbook » button. A bare word that is also a file's name is left in: it may be a column |
| Index on the chunks of a document (F-01, KBD-16) | `chunks_by_document` on `chunks (relative_path)`, created by the migration the first time an existing index is opened |
| The Analyse summary says what it did not read (F-20 line, KBD-30) | `IndexSummary.unreadFormats`: a count per extension of the files no reader opens; operating-system files, workbooks and files without an extension are not counted. The extractors for these formats are batch 4, after the owner's product decision |

## Settled by KB fix 4, batch 2, names and organisations (12 October 2026)

Branch `fix/kb-names-and-organisations`, cut from `kb-after-fix-04-batch-1`; fixes F-06, F-07, F-08, F-09, F-10 and F-04 of the battery (`docs/test-reports/knowledge-base-pass-1/04-final-report.md`, section 9). Report and figures: `docs/test-reports/knowledge-base-pass-1/lots/fix-04-batch-2-names-and-organisations.md`. Each fix was proved by a test seen failing first (`apps/desktop/src-tauri/tests/knowledge_fix_names.rs`).

| Subject | Decision |
| --- | --- |
| The extractor generation (all fixes) | `CURRENT_KB_VERSION` is 3 and the text extractor is version 2: documents and workbooks analysed before are read again from their stored text at the next Analyse the user starts, never in the background. The Analyse summary says so through its existing line (« documents déjà analysés relus pour y repérer les noms, sans solliciter l'IA ») |
| Organisations listed by name (F-06, KBD-27) | A pack may list well-known organisations (`org_names`: a full name and its short forms, one word each). The list is **type vocabulary**: every shipped pack's list is read whatever modules are active, like titles and markers (a module never changes a type). A listed organisation is trusted at once (method `organization_known`, 0.80); the entity carries the **full name** and its short form as an alias, so `CHUV` and `Centre hospitalier universitaire vaudois` are one entity. A short form is matched in the case the pack writes it (`hug` is an English word); a one-word name that is also an ordinary word (`Assura`) is a name only away from the start of a sentence. Lists: `health-ch` (hospitals, insurers, offices), `health-fr` (HCL, URSSAF, ARS, MGEN...), `accounting-fr` (EDF, ENGIE) |
| Unlisted acronyms are not read (F-06) | The battery proposed "an acronym of 3 to 6 capitals is an organisation candidate". Rejected: a practice's documents hold `IRM`, `TSH`, `ECG`, `CRP`, `TVA`, `PDF`, `IBAN`; two files that agree promote a candidate to active, so each would become an active organisation. Only a listed acronym is an organisation |
| Lower case words after a marker (F-06) | After a word that opens an organisation (`Hopitaux`, `Clinique`, `Association`, `Etude`), up to two lower case words of four letters or more are the organisation's own when a capitalised name (directly, or after particles) follows (`Hopitaux universitaires de Zurich`, `Clinique privee Bois-Cerf`). Names with no capitalised word (`Societe vaudoise de medecine`) are in the packs |
| Edges of an organisation (F-07, KBD-28) | An ampersand between two capitalised words joins them (`Garage Martin & Fils SARL`, `Etude Chappuis & Associes`); a person's name never crosses it. A sentence-initial preposition (`Entre`, `Chez`, `Pour`...: stop-words of the base) and a sentence-initial article or particle (`La`, `Le`, `Du`: not name-like when they open a sentence) are not the first word of a name; an elided article before a marker (`l'Etude`) is not part of the name. The name of an organisation known by its legal form is also an alias **without** it (`Dupont Immobilier`), so the next letter does not file it as a person |
| Streets and places are not people (F-07, KBD-11) | The base pack lists address markers (`rue`, `avenue`, `place`, `chemin`... / `street`, `road`...). A run of capitalised words that opens with one or stands right after one (looking back over particles) is not a person: `14 rue Jean Jaures`, `place de l'Hotel de Ville`, `boulevard du Docteur Bouchet`. The battery's "type an untitled run as `term`" is **not** done: a person met in a sentence stays a person candidate (a person is a person) |
| A name alone on a line (F-08, KBD-31) | The recipient of a letter, two or three capitalised words alone on a line, is read when the line above is a salutation alone (`Madame,`, `Madame, Monsieur,`: method `block_name`, 0.60, trusted at once) or when the line below opens with a number or an address marker (a candidate). A heading, a street, an organisation, a line in capitals, a line with a digit or a comma never qualifies |
| A name split by a line break (F-08, KBD-32) | The chunker removes blank lines, so a paragraph is not visible. A break is read as a space only when the text is a **wrapped paragraph** (at least three lines that stop at the margin, 85 % of the longest line and at least 60 characters), the line before it has five words with a lower case letter, ends on a title or a name, and the next line goes on with a capitalised word. A letterhead or an invoice (one long line) is never joined: the sandbox showed `Annemasse Solution` and `Annemasse Tel` when the rule was looser |
| Particles and apostrophes inside a name (F-08, F-09) | Up to two particles in a row (`de la Fontaine`, `van den Berg`). `D'`, `N'` and `O'` before a capital (`D'Amico`, `N'Diaye`, `O'Brien`) belong to the surname in the normaliser, the extractor and the question reader (one function, `apostrophe_joins_a_name`); `L'` and a lower case `d'` stay articles. This settles Q054 and Q081: a question for `Giuseppe D'Amico` can no longer resolve to `Helene D'Amico` through the surname alias `Amico` |
| Two orders of the same words (F-09, KBD-25) | `DUPONT Jean` (a surname in capitals before a word with lower case) is read given name first, in the resolver and when an entity is created, so it is `Jean Dupont` and never the person whose names are the reverse. **Plain case says nothing, inside one folder**: when `Lucas Roy` is known from a source of the same folder (documents, or tables) and another source of that folder writes `Roy Lucas`, that is a new entity with a possible match of reason `swapped_order` (in a question, a "did you mean"), never a link. That possible match is **not kept in a reduction**, and the lexical net does not search the words the other way round nor a surname that is the other's given name (`Roy`, `Monsieur Roy`): two people are not searched as one; **the tables keep the other order** (the appointments sheet that writes `Rochat Sophie` stays among the workbooks given to the engine for `Sophie Rochat`: the engine filters rows by the name). **Across folders** (a table that writes `Dupont Jean`, a letter that writes `Jean Dupont`) the two orders are the same person, which is how one person is usually written twice; the first measure without this rule showed 16 such duplicates in 170 persons |
| Demotion of a promoted candidate (F-10, KBD-12) | An entity that became active only because two sources named it carries `promoted_from_candidate`; when fewer than two sources name it any more (file deleted, changed, read again) it is a candidate again. An entity a pattern, a column, a listed organisation or the user made active is never demoted |
| The strongest method of a place is kept (F-10, KBD-13) | A name the base already knew that a title and a full name, an organisation marker or a prefix also found is recorded with that pattern's method, not as a hit on the list: the end-of-pass reading no longer rewrites the provenance. The title found with a known person is added to her names (`Dr Dupont`) even when another document named her first |
| A restored name has its table mention back (F-04, KBD-20) | Deleting an entity records the workbooks that named it; restoring it marks exactly those workbooks as read by an older extractor, so the next Data Analyse the user starts reads them again, with no change of the file. A tombstone made before this record existed makes every workbook due |

## Settled by KB fix 4, batch 3, table, router and check defects (12 October 2026)

Branch `fix/kb-retrieval-rules`, cut from `kb-after-fix-04-batch-2`; fixes F-12, F-13, F-15, F-22 and F-23 of the battery. The setting stays `suggest` (previous section), so F-17, F-18 and F-19 are not part of this batch. Report: `docs/test-reports/knowledge-base-pass-1/lots/fix-04-batch-3-table-router-and-check-defects.md`. Each fix was proved by a test seen failing first (`apps/desktop/src-tauri/tests/knowledge_fix_retrieval.rs`). Nothing here changes what the base reads: `CURRENT_KB_VERSION` stays 3.

| Subject | Decision |
| --- | --- |
| A word several values of a column share is no filter alone (F-12, KBD-24) | `Dr` in `Dr Jean Dupont` and `Dr Paul Martin`, a given name two people share, a surname two doctors share. `settle_shared_words` reads it with the other words of the question that name a value of the same column: a value those words picked holds the shared words, so `Dr Jean Dupont` counts like `Jean Dupont`; with nothing picked, the values that hold every shared word are an `In` filter (`Martin` alone is both Martins, shown as « medecin parmi ... »); a picked value that lacks a shared word (`Jean Martin`) keeps an `Equals` no cell matches, which is an honest zero |
| A date column no cell settles is still the file's calendar (F-12, KBD-22) | With no `Date` column and exactly one `ambiguous_date` column, a month, a year, a weekday, a period or a single written date is built on that column and refused by the engine with `non_date_column`, which names the column (`filter_column`; the client adds « Colonne concernée : ... »). It is never a filter on the text of its cells, and never dropped. A sheet with a settled date column is unchanged |
| « Which workbook? » is asked of a table question only (F-13, KBD-21) | In a mixed selection, `prepare_if_data_only` leaves the question to the mixed tier when it names the documents or the files (the folder router's own vocabulary: `names_the_documents`) unless a workbook is named, and when « which workbook » would be asked of a question the table classifier reads as neither an operation nor a column of any chosen workbook (`points_at_a_table`). `Combien de lignes ?` and a question that names a column still ask |
| The mixed tier does not show the choice of a workbook for a question that is not about a table (F-13, KBD-21; found by the owner's replay) | Leaving the data-only step was not enough: the mixed tier itself received « which workbook » from `prepare_for_mixed` and returned it as the whole answer, so the sentence and the « Demander à l'IA » button both ended on it. `prepare_for_mixed` now returns an unrecognised nudge (the sign a question the classifier could not read already leaves) for a question that is not about a table (`asks_about_a_table`), and `mixed_answer` reads it as « no table part »: the documents answer alone. A question that points at a table still asks |
| Questions about the documents in a mixed selection (F-13, KBD-23) | `commands::mixed_folder_tier`, before the data-only step: a question that names the documents and that the folder router answers from the inventory (count, list, tree) is answered with no model, about the selected documents (`folder_questions::answer_in_mixed_selection`). A question that names a file is never answered there, since the name can be a workbook of the Data Folder. Trade-off: `Combien de lignes dans tous les fichiers ?` now goes to the mixed tier |
| A reference with a folder and a name (F-23, KBD-02) | `mars/neurologie.pdf` names the file whose path ends that way **at a folder boundary** (reason `folder_suffix`). Two such files are asked about, never chosen; `ars/neurologie.pdf` and a folder the file is not under reach nothing |
| A delimiter is scored on the lines it cuts (F-15, KBD-14) | A title row above the header no longer makes a one-column table of a `;` file with no decimal comma. A prose note renamed `.csv` is still one column |
| A figure split by a line break (F-22, KBD-05) | The excerpts a model is checked against are read as written and with each line break taken for a space; the answer is read as before. A check can only gain a supported figure, never lose one |

## Settled by the owner for indexing and retrieval: the engine searches, the model only writes (14 October 2026)

| Subject | Decision |
| --- | --- |
| The principle | **The model is asked to write the answer and nothing else.** It is not used to search, list, count or rank when the index or the inventory can do it. Every step that finds, counts, ranks or cites is deterministic and precedes the model (`docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`, « Deterministic before generative »). |
| The router guard of `folder_questions.rs` (header, lines 13 to 19: a question that carries a content word goes to retrieval) | **Reversed for one family of sentences** (P-1, KBD-53): « Quels documents / courriers / résultats / factures parlent de, mentionnent, évoquent, viennent de, concernent X ? », « Y a-t-il des documents qui parlent de X ? », « Combien de documents mentionnent X ? ». These are answered from the full-text index (or from the entity base when X resolves to an entity) **with no model call and no embedding call**: the complete list of files, the count, one excerpt per file, the first 50 shown with the total. The answer **states that it is a search of the word X** (a synonym such as « assurance-maladie » for « LAMal » is not matched; the meaning search is kept for the other sentences). `knowledgeMaxCandidates` (15) never truncates a listing. |
| What keeps its road | A sentence that asks what a document says (`Que dit le courrier de Marie Dupont ?`, `Résume le dossier de Fatima Benali.`) is not a set question and still goes to retrieval and the model. A test pins each side. |
| Evidence | The lab (`docs/RETRIEVAL-LAB.md`, section 2.3): the application cites 36 of 111 expected files for the six set sentences, a plain phrase search of the index finds 111 of 111 with no wrong file. |
| Order of the work | The lexical channel is made precise and measured **before** the rank fusion (RRF), the rerank and the assembly (`docs/RETRIEVAL-LAB.md`, section 4). |
| The text re-indexing never touches the tabular data | A schema change touches only `documents`, `chunks`, `chunks_fts` and new tables; `tabular_inventories`, `tabular_workbooks` and `kb_column_semantics` are never dropped, cleared or rewritten; an automatic test checks it (`docs/RETRIEVAL-LAB.md`, section 7). |
| The AI machine's window | `ministral-3:3b=16384` (owner, 14 October 2026), from `docs/CONTEXT-BUDGET.md`. |

## Settled by the owner: the role of the indexing and search work, and the workspace target (14 October 2026, evening)

| Subject | Decision |
| --- | --- |
| Priority | **Indexing is the most important step**; search follows and rests on a perfect indexing of the documents. Supersedes the order of `docs/SESSION-LEXICAL-FIRST.md` section 5 (search road first). |
| Purpose of the work | The interface will be redefined as a **workspace: the files of the work folder on the left, a viewer of the opened files in the centre, the chat on the right.** **File search is the user's first step**, in the left list: a system to filter the *selected* files, those a question may process. This work therefore delivers **perfect indexing methods, then refined search methods, with no LLM, to filter efficiently the files of the scope.** Fixes are made with that interface in mind: results are structured (files, reasons, facets, coverage), not chat prose. |
| Entities | The Knowledge Base work is to be **used and deepened at indexing**: entities (with roles) read from the metadata of documents and from the spreadsheets, a file-to-entities view, entities per passage. A module still never changes an entity type. Design: `docs/LEXICAL-RETRIEVAL-DESIGN.md`. |
| Requests to the owner | Clear and precise, answerable in one line, with the recommended option marked. |
| D1 schema | **Yes**: new tables for the file profile; the index is rebuilt by the next Analyse; an old index is detected at launch and never rebuilt silently. |
| D2 spreadsheets | **Option B**: text values of cells searchable by word; names of people and organisations, places and identifiers included, numbers and dates excluded; cross-check with entities already known (`docs/LEXICAL-RETRIEVAL-DESIGN.md` 4.5). |
| D3 roles | **Yes**: `author`, `sender`, `recipient`, `subject`, `issuer` on existing entities, each with its evidence and confidence; precise and strategic. |
| D4 search fields | **Yes, to test**: a small search field inside the Documents Folder card and the Data Folder card, in place of the « utilisés » title line; the result **selects the files found**, which are then the only ones the chat considers (**superseded on 18 October 2026: a search no longer selects, see "Settled by the owner: the left panel becomes one file explorer"**). Long-term vision: **one unified file view and one search field in the left side panel.** Indexing is built first. |
| D5 types | Three levels: domain (documents / data), format (txt, docx, pdf, jpeg, png / xls, xlsx, csv), business type (letter, lab result, prescription, invoice, insurer statement, certificate, quote, meeting minutes, appointment letter, other); user-definable extra types. |
| D6 branches and substance | `feat/file-profiles` then `feat/file-finder`. The substance is an **irreproachable indexing method fitted to the existing code** (entities, metadata, formats, types), **including big documents of 1 000 pages** (to be handled next, `feat/long-documents`), then a deepened search built on it. |
| Method rules | No redundant Rust test runs: only the targeted test during work, the full suite once per branch; estimates must be explained and measured (`docs/LEXICAL-RETRIEVAL-DESIGN.md` 11). |
| Q1 to Q5 | Q1 **(a)**: clearing the field keeps the selection, the unselected files stay visible and the filter can be reset. Q2 **(a)**: search fields after the finder; **concentrate on indexing now**. Q3 yes (field replaces the title line, count and warnings kept). Q4 yes (data types: patient or client list, invoicing, accounting, schedule, stock, other). Q5 yes, **on the absolute condition that nothing is broken in the spreadsheet indexing, inventory and deterministic engine** (read-only for this work; new evidence in the profile tables; byte-identical snapshot and the same 100 % `data` replay as proof). |
| File name | The **file name is a first-class indexing criterion**: searchable words, prefixes, any order, the original name before cleaning, and the people, organisations, companies, types, dates and identifiers found in it (`docs/LEXICAL-RETRIEVAL-DESIGN.md` 5.3). |
| Status | No question open. Next: the owner's go to start step 1. No product code yet. |

## Taken while building the file finder, to confirm (14 October 2026)

| Subject | Decision |
| --- | --- |
| The kind noun of a set sentence | Only a **precise** noun filters by business type (`factures`, `ordonnances`, `devis`, `certificats`, `convocations`, `décomptes`, `résultats`, `lettres`). **`courrier(s)` does not**: measured on the 200-document sandbox, the filter `letter` lost 17 of the 111 files of the six set sentences (the insurers' statements and the other correspondence are typed otherwise), against 0 lost without it. The rank sentence uses the word `courrier` as a word of the file path instead. Data in `work-folder-questions/*.json` (`set_question.nouns`) |
| A set sentence is a whole-word search | The last word of a typed **sentence** is a whole word; in the **search field** it may be the start of a word (the field is used while typing). The answer says it is a word search: a synonym or another form of the word is not matched. X is 1 to 4 words, a quoted phrase (up to 8) or an identifier; an interrogative as X, any filter word, or a longer X keeps the sentence on its road |
| Identifiers | The user's number is folded to its compact form by the active packs, in the command (`find_files`) and in the set sentence, and met in the profile's stored compact form: the lab's AVS number written without dots is found (1 of 1), and so are the 37 distinct AVS numbers of the 200-document sandbox, written without separators, in all 48 files that hold them, with no wrong file. The **pinned-first rule on the ordinary chat road** (`LexicalOptions`) is **not built**: it can only be judged on the `identifier` family of the real stack (embeddings from the AI machine), which this work could not run |
| The panel's search | **Superseded on 18 October 2026 (a search only filters the view; selecting is a separate click, see "Settled by the owner: the left panel becomes one file explorer").** Typing a search **replaces the selection** with the analysed files found (owner D4); clearing the field or « Réinitialiser » ends the filter and keeps the selection; « Tous les fichiers » is a view. A file found and not analysed (or in a format the product does not read) is counted and never selected. The finder itself never changes a selection |
| Answers from supplied material | Sent at **temperature 0.1** (`GROUNDED_TEMPERATURE`): the answer from excerpts, the mixed answer and the tabular query plan; an open conversation sends none and keeps the gateway's default. Not 0: a model held at exactly zero can loop on a small machine |
| Page numbers in the figure check | The pages of the excerpts are known figures, but only as bare integers: `(page 12)` draws no alarm, `200.00` on a page 200 still does |
| The rank sentence | `Quel est le dernier courrier reçu ?` ranks the **readable** documents. Superseded in part on 14 October 2026 (next section): it now reads the profile's chosen document date, with its source, before the date of the clean name and the modification date, and filters by business type when the noun stands for one |
| Cost of a search | A finder call raises the connection's page cache to 64 MiB: with the default 2 MiB a folder of 20 000 files read the same pages again and again (250 to 420 ms against 50 to 120 ms). Measured in `tests/file_finder_bench.rs` |

## Settled while stabilising the file finder (14 October 2026)

Owner answers, in the work of `docs/SESSION-LD-1-FINDER-STABILISATION.md`:

| Subject | Decision |
| --- | --- |
| The search field in the folder cards | Stays **above** the « Documents utilisés » line, which keeps its count and its warning (owner, confirmed) |
| `courrier` | Stays a **word of the path**, never a business type: as a type it cost 17 of the 111 files (owner, confirmed) |
| The rank sentence | Replaced by the profile reading (below) (owner, confirmed) |
| Documents and tables both selected | « Quels documents parlent de X ? » **covers the tables too**: one list per folder, each stating its folder, from the profile's cell words, headers and names, no model (owner) |

Built, to confirm:

| Subject | Decision |
| --- | --- |
| Entities as X | When X is **exactly one** active entity of the knowledge base (the deterministic resolver in question mode, no selection restriction, so two people of one name anywhere in the base keep X a phrase), the answer is the files linked to it (the base's mentions and the profile's entity rows) **together with** the files that write X or one of its surfaces as phrases, the entity's name stated. The surfaces are the canonical name and its aliases of two words or more, without a surname alone, a title form, an initial or an abbreviation (several people carry those), six at most. The port is `finder::names::NameResolver`, given to `set_search::answer_with_names`: it sits beside the router, which still receives only `CorpusWords`. A quoted phrase and an identifier are never resolved. Measured: the six set sentences stay 111 of 111, 0 wrong file |
| The rank sentence from the profile | `CorpusWords::profiles()` (default none) gives the router a `ProfileFacts` port, implemented by the index with one query. A date comes from the profile's chosen document date with its recorded source (`header_label`, `header_dated`, `header_title`, `header_text`, `filename`, `metadata_created`), else the clean name's date, else the modification date (`modified`). A profile of other content than the file's is not read. A noun that stands for a business type filters by the profile's type; any other noun, and a file with no profile, by the words of the path. The warning about mixed dates is given only when a modification date stands among dates of documents |
| User-defined business types | Manual rows in `file_profile_user_types`, written by no Analyse and kept by a folder's Reset; every code starts with `user_` and the label is the row's. A user type is specific, weighed by the rules of `types.rs`. The vocabulary hash includes them, so the next Analyse builds the profiles again. **Re-evaluating** reads the stored profile only (name, title, header words, sheet and column names; not the body, which the profile does not keep) and never a file: a shipped type changes only when a user type beats it under the same rules. The editing screen and the commands are built in tab 3 |
| The user's own names | A settings key `ownNames` (a list), edited in `settings.json` until the dialog gets its « Avancé » group, cleaned on save. They enter `ProfileVocabulary::with_user_names` at every Analyse, and the vocabulary hash, so a property that holds one of them never makes the user the author of a file and the profiles are built again when the list changes. **Kept on purpose, to be used soon (owner, 14 October 2026):** the product will have several client workstations on one network and an authentication on the server, so that conversations can be saved per user. The authenticated user's name is then the natural source of this list, per workstation, so that **the user's own name is taken out of the entities and out of the search** (today the list only stops a property from making her the author of a file; it does not yet remove her from the knowledge base's entities nor from the search, which is the step to build with the authentication). Do not remove this setting |
| Test `a_refusal_of_the_request_itself_is_never_retried` | Its replies were immediate yet it ran under the 250 ms embedding deadline: on a loaded machine the deadline passed before the reply was read, the refusal became a timeout and was retried. It, and the sibling that retries an unavailable server, now run under the 10 s deadline; a deadline is only short in the tests that test it |

## Settled for long documents and the two-speed Analyse (14 October 2026)

Owner answers, in the work of `docs/SESSION-LD-2-INGESTION.md` (figures and reasoning: `docs/LONG-DOCUMENTS.md`):

| Subject | Decision |
| --- | --- |
| The "long" threshold | More than **60 000 characters** of extracted text, whatever the format (owner, decision 1). Below it the chunker is today's, byte for byte (golden test of 146 documents) |
| An index from before | Detected by one lookup, left untouched until « Analyser »; the Analyse upgrades the layout **inside the pass**, copying passages and vectors as they are, and reads again a document only if its structure or chunker version is older, reusing the vectors of unchanged passages (owner, decision 2) |
| Progress display | Two lines during the pass, « Lecture des documents » then « Recherche par sens : x % »; on the card only while below 100 % (owner, decision 3) |
| Chunk size | **1200 characters, no overlap, no context prefix** for documents above the threshold, chosen on the real-model grid (owner, decision 4). The overlap did not raise any channel and cost Analyse time; 800 and 1600 were not better; the prefix lowered the meaning-based channels |
| PDF outline | `lopdf` with our own walk of the tree (owner, decision 5); `Document::get_toc` loses repeated titles and garbles accents |
| Readers postponed on 13 October (ODT, RTF, EML, VTT, ODP, PPTX, ODS) | Stay postponed (owner, decision 6) |
| Cleaning of short documents (headers and footers, cut words) | **Not done** for now, so that they stay byte-identical (owner) |

Built, to confirm:

| Subject | Decision |
| --- | --- |
| Where a vector may be absent | `chunks.embedding` is nullable in an index made by this version (NULL, never an empty blob); a document's structure, prefixes and cards live in additive tables (`doc_structure`, `doc_sections`, `chunk_sections`, `chunk_index_text`, `document_cards`, `ocr_page_cache`, `kb_source_truncated`, two profile tables) |
| Order of the pass | Phase 1 for every file (text, word index, structure, names), then the knowledge-base backfill and the profiles, then the cards, then phase 2 (the cards' vectors first, then the passages', 16 at a time and 20 000 characters, one transaction a batch). A stop is honoured between files, batches and scanned pages; the next pass reads nothing again |
| A file the server refuses | Its text stays in the index (searchable by words, its names kept); only its vectors wait. A failing batch is replayed file by file to find the file; three refused files in a row, or a server that is gone, stop the pass with the gateway's own error, everything kept. This replaces "a failed file leaves no trace" (9 tests rewritten) |
| An extraction error | Names the file (`extraction_failed`) and the pass goes on; it used to abort the whole pass |
| A scan | Each recognised page is kept in `ocr_page_cache` (ten pages a write) and used by the next pass; dropped when the document is stored or the file changes |
| Document cards | One per document, built with no model after the profiles; fixed English labels; **not a passage** (own table, own word index, own vector); embedded first. Their type, date and roles come from the profile |
| Locator | `Evidence.ordinal` (rank inside the page) and `Evidence.section` (title and pages); the finder's `Locator { page, ordinal, sectionLabel }`; a passage belongs to the section in effect where it starts. A hit also carries its places (at most 20) and the number of matching passages; the set answer lists the pages |
| Ranking | Reads the vectors of the scope one row at a time and the text of the few kept; same scores and order as before (parity test, 360 comparisons). The restriction of vector scoring to other channels' candidates and int8 vectors were **not** applied: not needed at 100 000 passages (755 ms, 9 MB) |
| Embedding model | Recorded with its dimension; a pass with another model, or vectors of another length under the same name, recomputes every vector in the pass; a search whose question has another length than the stored vectors returns `mismatched_chunks` instead of comparing silently |
| Knowledge-base caps | Half as many names and five times as many mentions as passages, floors 500 and 5 000, ceilings 20 000 and 100 000; a source over its cap is marked and the entity sheet says so |
| Extraction and the executor | `block_in_place` on the multi-threaded runtime (borrowed ports cannot move into `spawn_blocking`), inline on a current-thread runtime |


## Restart point of 16 October 2026: everything after `fe15e03` is set aside (owner)

**Decided by the owner on 16 October 2026.** The application restarts from the state of commit `fe15e03` (branch `feat/long-documents-ingestion-search`: the file finder, the long-document ingestion in two speeds, the stop button, the vector share and the located sources). **The work done after it was not satisfactory and is not built on.** It stays in git, unmerged, as a reference to read and never as a base. **The branches were deleted on 19 October 2026 in a branch clean-up; the commits stay reachable through three archive tags** (`archive/set-aside-tab4` at `d3e8eec`, `archive/set-aside-search` at `36366ea`, `archive/set-aside-semantic-selection` at `3549ac0`), pushed to `origin`. The "Branch" column below gives the original branch name; read a commit with `git show <hash>` or `git log <tag>`:

| Commit | Branch | What it holds |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `4e6a778` | `feat/tab4-note-facts-readers` (tag `archive/set-aside-tab4`) | lot A: end page of a passage, marked words, coverage line |
| `1d8a13b` | same | configurable gateway address, optional GPU override, `docs/INSTALL-SERVER.md` |
| `36366ea` | same (and `search/integration`, tag `archive/set-aside-search`) | lots B to H: phrase search, pinned identifier, refusal, answer checks, « Passages trouvés », hand-started summaries, finder filters, the reranker service |
| `d3e8eec` | `feat/tab4-note-facts-readers` | one "to check" note, summary checks, removal of the unused word methods, dates and amounts as facts, the new readers, the approximate-index measure |
| `3549ac0` | `test/semantic-selection` (was local only; tag `archive/set-aside-semantic-selection`) | selection of the excerpts by meaning |

**Consequences.** (1) Nothing from these commits is merged into the restart line by `git merge` or `git cherry-pick`; a piece is taken back only when the owner asks for it, rebuilt on the restart state and tested there. (2) The first piece taken back is **the readers**: ODT, ODP, PPTX, RTF, EML, VTT, **SRT** (subtitles, added by the owner on 16 October) and ODS on the tabular side (postponed on 13 October, first built on 15 October inside `d3e8eec`); the brief is `docs/SESSION-READERS-RESTART.md` (local handoff). (3) The documents `docs/RETRIEVAL-QUALITY.md` and the tab 3 and 4 sections of this file exist only on the set-aside branches: they are not part of the restart state and must not be quoted as if they were.

**The owner's requests of 16 October 2026 for the readers and the test.** (a) SRT joins the readers (a line per cue with its start time, one cue parser shared with VTT). (b) The human test is a **large, documented, reproducible test**: at least **1 000 text documents and 500 spreadsheet files**, **including every new format** (VTT, SRT, RTF, EML, ODT, ODP, PPTX for documents; ODS with XLSX and CSV for tables); at least **300 questions: 150 on documents, 100 on spreadsheets, 50 mixed** (each needing a document and a spreadsheet together); runnable on **different AI models** with the same library, questions and scoring; and it must **demonstrate every feature of the application** as it stands on the restart state. The library and the question book extend `scripts/gen-knowledge-sandbox.py` through a new profile (the existing outputs stay byte-identical); the library is generated into `data/` (never versioned), a small slice is versioned; results go to `data/large-test/<date>-<model>/`; the protocol is `docs/test-reports/large-test/README.md`. The agent walks it on the 200-document library and the small slice, and runs the full library once.

## Settled by the readers rebuilt on the restart state (16 October 2026)

Branch `feat/new-readers` (deleted on 19 October 2026 after its merge; tag `milestone/new-readers`), cut from `61c30a5`. Eight formats, one at a time, each proved by its own tests: VTT and SRT (one cue parser, `readers::cues`, shared), RTF, EML, ODT, ODP, PPTX, and ODS on the tabular side. Nothing was taken from the set-aside branches by `merge` or `cherry-pick`; the first version in `d3e8eec` was read, reviewed line by line and rebuilt. They are **readers, never converters**: text is extracted, no file is changed, moved or deleted.

| Subject | Decision |
| --- | --- |
| Where a reader lives | `src/readers/` (`mod.rs` dispatches by extension; `extraction::extract` falls through to it for any extension it does not handle itself). `discovery::SUPPORTED_EXTENSIONS`, `FileKind::from_extension` (`vtt srt rtf eml` are `document_text`, `odt odp pptx` are `document_office`, `ods` is `tabular_candidate`) and `mime_type_for` list them; a test requires every extension of `readers::EXTENSIONS` to be discovered |
| Pages | Subtitles, RTF, EML, ODT: one page. ODP and PPTX: **one page per slide**; PPTX in the order the deck lists them (`ppt/presentation.xml` and its relationships), numbered order when the deck does not say; speaker notes, pictures, charts and diagrams are not read. ODT leaves out comments and tracked deletions |
| What was added to the reference version | RTF: `\bin` binary data skipped, UTF-16 surrogate pairs, consecutive `\'hh` bytes decoded together (two-byte code pages). EML: a file with no header field is empty, not guessed at; numeric and common named HTML entities; headers decoded as UTF-8 or Windows-1252. PPTX: the `Fallback` of an `AlternateContent` is not read twice. Every zip part is capped at 64 MiB unpacked |
| ODS | `TabularFormat::Ods`; the same `calamine` adapter as the XLSX, which refuses an archive with no `META-INF/manifest.xml`. The spreadsheet tables, the engine and the XLSX/CSV tests are unchanged |
| F-20 (KBD-30) | The folder card line « n fichiers n'ont pas été lus » stops counting these formats: it keys on `FileKind::Unsupported`. On the 200-document sandbox the unread files fall from 21 to 1 (the old binary `.ppt`); `tests/knowledge_fix_swiss_first.rs` and the other tests that used `.odt .rtf .eml .vtt` as "a file nobody reads" now use `.pages .wps .msg .key` |
| Clean names | A file in one of these formats is renamed to a clean name by the Analyse like any document (the 25 September rule); content is never touched |
| Sandbox truth | `fixtures/knowledge-sandbox` is unchanged (its `readable` flags describe the older generator). `tests/common/validation.rs::Truth::load` marks the files of the new formats readable and recomputes `relevant_readable`, so the six set sentences now expect **117** files (111 before: six files of the 200 are in the new formats), find 117, and list no wrong file. The published figures of lot 11 are history and were not rewritten |
| Visibility | `tabular::question::{has_writing_intent, has_distributive_word}` became `pub` so that the large test can replay the mixed tier's dispatch from outside the crate |
| Not done, reported to the owner | The folder questions (`resources/work-folder-questions/*.json`, `extensions`) know pdf, texte, markdown, word, png and jpeg only: « Combien de fichiers vtt ? » is not read as a question about an extension and returns the total of analysed documents. Adding the seven extensions is a data change in the router; it was not made because the brief forbids changing the finder or the router without a report first |
| Limits | RTF tables come out as tab-separated cells; EML attachments are not read; a password-protected or damaged archive is a parse failure named in the summary; UTF-16 subtitle files are not read; no OCR on the new formats |

The large reproducible test (`docs/test-reports/large-test/README.md`) is built on these readers: a generated library of 1 251 documents and 510 spreadsheets, a book of 300 questions, a runner and a scorer.

## Settled by the resizable three-zone layout (17 October 2026)

Owner decisions of the workspace series, built on `feat/resizable-panels`. Interface and rules: `docs/CLIENT.md`, "Layout".

| Subject | Decision |
| --- | --- |
| Library | `react-resizable-panels` **4.14.5**, the v4 API (`Group`, `Panel`, `Separator`). **A number is pixels, a string is a percentage.** Pixel minimums (Resources 240, Workspace 320, Chat 340), percentage defaults (22 / 38 / 40 open, 26 / 0 / 74 closed). No other dependency, no icon library |
| The centre | An extensible slot: `WorkspaceView | null` held by `App`, `openWorkspace` / `closeWorkspace`. **Collapsed at launch**, its separator hidden while collapsed. The panel is **always rendered** (size 0 when closed) so the chat is never remounted. Visible exactly while a view is held |
| Window | `minWidth` raised from 900 to **1000**: the three minimums, the separators and the padding need about 960. Height unchanged |
| Two stored layouts | `settings.json` `layout`: `open` and `collapsed`, each a triple of percentages, the centre above 0 in the first and exactly 0 in the second. Saved only at the end of a gesture the user made, kept valid in Rust (`PanelLayouts::sanitised`) on load **and** on save, reset with the other settings |
| Settings in Rust, not browser storage | The project uses no `localStorage`; the layout is a convenience that follows the same file, the same sanitising and the same reset as every other setting |
| Save queue | `save_settings` replaces the whole file, so two saves started from the same copy overwrote each other (it happened at every launch: the locale and the alias self-heal both saved from one snapshot). `useAppSettings` now serialises its saves, merges each change into the settings of its turn, lets a failed save pass, and routes `reset` and the initial load through the same queue. `update` is stable between renders |
| Library facts confirmed by running it (Edge 1180 and 1000 px wide) | The layout is an object keyed by panel id; `onLayoutChanged(layout, { isUserInteraction })` is called once per gesture; keyboard resizing is 5 % per arrow, Home and End go to the limits, Enter on a separator collapses a collapsible panel beside it, a double click resets to the default size; a panel's limits change **after** the effect that follows a render, so a layout set in the same effect is cut back (it is applied one frame later) |

The words « Données utilisées » and « data list » still say data |
