| --- | --- | --- |
| N-5 | The 1.7b model copies the English card and profile text into its French answer (franglais). | Not investigated; candidate remedy in step 07 (one instruction sentence, with its guard). |
| N-6 | The hidden interpreter read « répartis les montants par fournisseur » as a count of rows per supplier. | Pre-existing, kept. |
| N-7 | The model wrote `=JOIN(...)`, which is not a spreadsheet function. | Step 07's checker. |


## Taken while building TABULAR step 06 (23 October 2026), to confirm

Step 06 replaces the one button under a computed figure by the two actions the owner decided on 20-21 October 2026 (plan decisions 11 and 12): **"Expliquer le calcul"** and **"Demander a l'IA"** (the analysis). The code is in `calculation_follow_up.rs` (new, Tauri-free), `tabular/escalation.rs` (`format_calculation_record`), `tabular_answer.rs` (`calculation_record`, `sheet_sizes`, `replay_model_plan`), `mixed_answer.rs` (`ANALYSIS_ADDENDUM`, `CalculationSide`, `build_turn_for`, `table_evidence_text`), `commands.rs` (`calculation_follow_up_tier`, `advisory_tier`, `mixed_tier`) and, on the interface side, `src/lib/calculationFollowUp.ts`, `useChat.ts`, `MessageList.tsx` and `ipc.ts`. Record: `docs/test-reports/tabular-named-files/step-06-explain-and-analyse-calculation.md`.

**Supersedes** the 1 October 2026 row of "Settled by the session 14 manual validation pass, round three" (« No way to ask the model anyway, when a classified answer looks wrong »): the button that made the model read the question again is gone **for an answer that carries a figure**. It stays, unchanged, for an answer with no figure (a nudge listing columns), where it is the only way left to try to understand the question. The row « A failed "Ask AI" leaves a trace (BUG-18) » therefore concerns a nudge only.

| # | Choice | Reason | Guarantee kept |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | **The figure is computed again by the program and compared, never taken from the interface.** The request carries the original question, the scope the answer was given under (kept on the entry when the answer arrived, so a selection changed since changes nothing) and the figure on screen (`file`, `value`, `derivation`). `calculation_follow_up::recompute` narrows that selection to the shown file (so a knowledge-base narrowing at the first answer cannot become « which workbook? » now), computes with `prepare` (with documents ticked also `prepare_for_mixed`, because the figure may come from door 3 or door 4) and accepts only a result whose file and value are equal to the shown ones. Anything else (a changed or removed file, another figure, no figure) is the existing `scope_unavailable`, with no model call. | A confident explanation of a figure the file no longer gives would be the worst answer. Equality of two `f64` computed by the same code on the same data is exact. | Every figure shown is computed by the engine; no model call before the comparison. |
| 2 | **A figure the hidden interpreter read is replayed from its stored plan** (`tabular_answer::replay_model_plan`): the interface sends the derivation back, the plan goes through `query_plan::resolve` and `engine::execute` as the first time, and the model is never asked again. A plan that no longer parses or resolves (an unknown column, an unknown field) is refused. | The plan is untrusted JSON on its way back. | A tampered plan cannot produce a figure the engine would not produce. |
| 3 | **The calculation record** (`escalation::format_calculation_record(&CalculationRecord)`, plan 4.6) carries: the question (for « Expliquer » only), the workbook (plain path, and the folder when it is the other family's), the sheet and the header row, the operation in words with the column and the unit, the reading the model proposed when it was the hidden interpreter (alias only, never the plan), `Understood as` (the existing filter vocabulary), `Rows: N kept out of M data rows`, `Left out: U values in column ... that could not be read as numbers (counted over the whole column)`, and the result. No column letter (the locator holds none), no cell and no row; group labels and distinct values appear in `Result` exactly as `format_evidence` already sent them. | « Make the record carry only what the engine holds today »: steps 10, 15 and 17 add a field and a line. | No cell value or row reaches a model through the record (a test asserts it on a real file). |
| 4 | **« Expliquer le calcul » is one gateway call** (`calculation_follow_up::explain`) with `EXPLAIN_INSTRUCTION` (the wording of plan 4.7, kept) and the record: no history, no cards, no sample, no document, no embedding. The text streams into `ChatEntry.explanation`, never into `content`. The reply is checked with `number_check::unsupported_numbers` against the record and the question (two digits or more) and with `arithmetic_expressions`, and the same lines as for other answers are written beside it (`chat.explanationUnverifiedNumber`, `chat.modelArithmetic`). A model that cannot be reached, or writes nothing, is an `unavailable` explanation (a sentence under the figure), not an error; only her own stop is an error and keeps what was written. | The figure must stay whatever the AI machine does. | The explanation is the model's text under the program's figure, set apart on screen and in the copy. |
| 5 | **« Demander a l'IA » is the writer of the mixed tier with the record in its `Table results` block and `ANALYSIS_ADDENDUM` ended onto the instruction** (`build_turn_for(.., analysis)`): `advisory_tier` with `PendingAnswer::Done(figure)` when only tables are ticked, `mixed_tier(.., known_figure)` when documents are ticked as well. The zero-call guarantee for a computed value with no document is lifted **for the analysis only**; `verify_numbers` is given the table block as a known text, so a row count or a filter value the model quotes from the record is not corrected, and `reject_citations` is unchanged. When the model fails, the figure stays (a `MixedAnswer` with an empty answer and `gateway_unavailable`), never replaced by the nudge. | The model starts from the engine's figure and never computes a new one. | An ordinary computed figure still answers with no model call. |
| 6 | **Buttons** (pure `buttonsFor`, tested): a computed value in tables only and the data-only answer of door 3 get « Expliquer le calcul » and « Demander a l'IA »; a nudge keeps its one « Demander a l'IA » (old meaning); a structural fact and a human choice get none (plan section 8, point 8: not decided here); a mixed answer written by the model that carries a computed value keeps « Regenerer » and gets « Expliquer le calcul », **not** the analysis (it would be a second « Regenerer »); a tables-only advisory answer keeps the one « Regenerer ». **Defaults to confirm:** the figure a mixed answer shows alone (the model wrote nothing: documents unavailable or the AI machine stopped) is the two-button case, because there is no prose to regenerate. | Plan decisions 11 and 12 and the prompt's matrix. | - |
| 7 | **An analysis is an ordinary mixed answer, flagged** (`calculation.analysed`): « Regenerer » under it asks for the analysis of the same figure under the same selection again, not for the plain figure. | Otherwise the button would undo the analysis. | - |
| 8 | **`force_model` no longer applies to a classified figure:** in `answer_sync`, `skip_deterministic` / `force_model` returns `TryModel` only when the deterministic answer is not a value. Callers of `prepare(.., force_model)`: `commands::tabular_tier` (the user's flag: now effective for a nudge only), `answer()`, `prepare_if_data_only`, `advisory_tier` (degrade), `calculation_follow_up::recompute` (all `false`), and two tests that asked for `true` (changed: `forcing_the_model_on_a_figure_no_longer_re_reads_the_question` in `tests/tabular_query_plan.rs`, `ask_ai_on_a_figure_keeps_the_computed_value_and_does_not_ask_the_model` in `tests/tabular_hap1.rs`). The mixed door 0 (`skip_deterministic` in a mixed selection) is unchanged: it serves a nudge and « Regenerer ». | Owner decision 11. | A nudge keeps its old meaning (a test asserts both). |
| 9 | **The request keeps one new optional parameter, not an overload:** `ask_with_sources(.., calculation_follow_up, on_event)`; in `askWithSources` it lives in a trailing options object (`AskOptions`, the tenth parameter), where step 11 adds `tableCarryOver`. `sourced_answer` tests it before the tier match (the follow-up bypasses the doors on purpose). | Plan 4.7. | - |
| 10 | **`MixedContext` has one new field, `calculation: CalculationSide`** (`sheet_sizes`, `analysis`; `Default` is the ordinary turn), in its eight places; `build_turn` is `#[cfg(test)]` and production uses `build_turn_for`. `table_evidence_text` is `pub(crate)` and takes the sheet sizes. | The record needs the data rows of the sheet. | The turn without an analysis is the earlier turn byte for byte (a test asserts it). |
| 11 | **The trace gains `explained`** (`WorkbookUseHow::Explained`, `Answered::Explained(file)`, `workbookUse.explained` in both catalogues): informative, not a caution; it adds no aggregate line and the interface replaces an earlier `explained` entry when the button is pressed again. | Plan 4.3. | - |
| 12 | **The explanation sits under the figure and above the notes,** with its own title and a lighter rule; it is part of the copied answer and of what read-aloud reads, after the figure. A stop keeps what was written; an empty block goes. Pressing the button again replaces the block. | « Added under it », « replaces the block, not stacks ». | - |
| 13 | **Corrections after the owner's test of step 06 (23 October 2026):** (a) the `explained` trace line is not written when the explanation could not be written (it said the AI had explained the calculation with the AI stopped); (b) one sentence added to `EXPLAIN_INSTRUCTION` (write in the language of the question, in your own words, do not copy the lines of the record), because the 1.7b model copied « 8 kept out of 8 data rows » and « Workbook » into its French text (the same echo as N-5); not measured on the models, to be seen at the next test; (c) **« Demander a l'IA » under a nudge in a mixed selection now reaches the interpreter:** the mixed tier hands any refusal back with no model call, so the button returned the same nudge, a « the AI wrote no answer » line and an unrelated document source (part F2 of her test; the behaviour is older than step 06). `commands::data_side_is_a_nudge` (the data-only answer is a nudge, `prepare_if_data_only` = `Some(Done(Nudge))`) sends the press to `tabular_tier` with `skip_deterministic`, which reads the data selection only and never the documents: the meaning the button has with tables alone. The result is a tabular answer, so its own buttons apply. | Option 1 chosen by the owner. | A figure is never re-read; only a refusal is retried. |
